- **C002**: 提交训练/评估任务时必须在命令前设置 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=X，禁止依赖 device_map="auto" 自动分配 GPU（原因：plan_011 和 rsft_ced_s45_train_r3 两次因未设 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 导致 device_map="auto" 跨 GPU 分布，造成 OOM 和资源冲突。已知 bug 重复发生。）
- **C003**: 提交训练任务时必须在命令前设置 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=X，禁止依赖 device_map="auto" 自动分配 GPU（原因：plan_011 和 rsft_ced_s45_train_r3 均因未设 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 导致模型跨 GPU 分布，造成 OOM 或显存冲突。已重复发生两次。）

- **C005**: no-evidence 模型 eval 必须传 --no_evidence 参数，确保推理时使用 NO_EVIDENCE_SYSTEM_PROMPT 与训练一致（原因：plan_006 1.7B/8B no-evidence eval 漏传该参数，导致训练/推理提示词不匹配（模型被要求输出未训练过的 evidence 字段），结果全部无效。Reviewer critical #20。）

&lt;/project_context>
**任务**：深度搜索 canonical 4B CED RSFT s42 checkpoint 路径。之前两次搜索未找到。

**背景**：
- 项目 MVP2 阶段训练了多个 4B RSFT 变体，registry 中 `mvp2_rsft_train_r1` 是最早的 RSFT 实验
- 已确认 `rsft_output_random_k1` 是 random baseline（非 CED），`rsft_output_ced_s43` 是 seed=43
- 需要找到 seed=42 的 CED-scored RSFT checkpoint（如果还存在的话）

**搜索步骤**（在 rtx5090-4x 服务器上，account: root）：

1. 更宽范围搜索：
   ```bash
   ls /root/autodl-tmp/ | grep -i rsft
   find /root/autodl-tmp -maxdepth 3 -name "adapter_model.safetensors" 2>/dev/null
   ```

2. 搜索含 r1、r2a、r2b 的目录（MVP2 RSFT 变体命名）：
   ```bash
   find /root/autodl-tmp -maxdepth 3 -type d -name "*r1*" -o -name "*r2a*" -o -name "*r2b*" 2>/dev/null
   ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/ | grep -E "rsft|r1|r2"
   ```

3. 搜索无后缀的 `rsft_output/`：
   ```bash
   ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output/ 2>/dev/null || echo "NOT FOUND"
   ```

4. 查看训练脚本中的 output_dir：
   ```bash
   grep -r "output_dir" /root/autodl-tmp/freige/scripts/ 2>/dev/null | grep -i rsft | head -20
   grep -r "rsft_output" /root/autodl-tmp/freige/scripts/ 2>/dev/null | head -20
   ```

5. 查看 mvp2_rsft_train_r1 相关的训练命令/脚本：
   ```bash
   find /root/autodl-tmp/freige/scripts -name "*rsft*" -o -name "*mvp2*" 2>/dev/null
   cat 找到的相关脚本
   ```

6. 搜索 rsft 训练数据目录（可能暗示 checkpoint 位置）：
   ```bash
   find /root/autodl-tmp -maxdepth 3 -type d -name "*rsft_scored*" -o -name "*rsft_train*" -o -name "*rsft_candidates*" 2>/dev/null
   ```

**产出**：简短报告，结论是：
- 找到了（路径 + 验证 adapter 完整性 + seed 确认）
- 或确认不存在（列出所有搜索过的路径，推测被清理的时间/原因）

如果确认不存在，检查是否有已保存的训练数据（`rsft_scored_*/rsft_train.jsonl`）可以用来重新训练。

Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps.
